49As well as being part of the PCRE2 library, this file is #included by the
50pcre2test program, which redefines the PRIV macro to change table names from

52just one of these tables is actually needed. When compiling the library, some
53headers are needed. */

62/* The tables herein are needed only when UCP support is built, and in PCRE2
63that happens automatically with UTF support. This module should not be
64referenced otherwise, so it should not matter whether it is compiled or not.
65However a comment was received about space saving - maybe the guy linked all
66the modules rather than using a library - so we include a condition to cut out
67the tables when not needed. But don't leave a totally empty module because some
68compilers barf at that. Instead, just supply some small dummy tables. */

421/* These are the main two-stage UCD tables. The fields in each record are:
422script (8 bits), character type (8 bits), grapheme break property (8 bits),
423offset to multichar other cases or zero (8 bits), offset to other case or zero
424(32 bits, signed), bidi class (5 bits) and script extension (11 bits) packed
425into a 16-bit field, and offset in binary properties table (16 bits). */
